  var currentWeek = WEEKS_DATA[selectedWeekIndex] || WEEKS_DATA[1];
  var nextSlide = function nextSlide() {
    return setCurrentSlide(function (prev) {
      return (prev + 1) % GALLERY_SLIDES.length;
    });
  };
  var prevSlide = function prevSlide() {
    return setCurrentSlide(function (prev) {
      return (prev - 1 + GALLERY_SLIDES.length) % GALLERY_SLIDES.length;
    });
  };
  var scrollToSection = function scrollToSection(id) {
    setActiveNav(id);
    if (currentView !== 'site') setCurrentView('site');
    var element = document.getElementById(id);
    if (element) element.scrollIntoView({
      behavior: 'smooth'
    });
  };
  var switchRole = function switchRole(roleKey) {
    if (roleKey === 'dono') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: 'Luis Valverde',
        email: 'luis@valverds.com',
        role: 'dono',
        barberKey: 'luis',
        title: 'Barbeiro & Dono',
        avatar: 'luis_valverde.jpg'
      });
      setCurrentView('reception');
      setReceptionTab('agenda');
    } else if (roleKey === 'barbeiro') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: 'Yago Barber',
        email: 'yago@valverds.com',
        role: 'barbeiro',
        barberKey: 'yago',
        title: 'Barbeiro',
        avatar: 'yago_barber.png'
      });
      var yagoSched = barberSchedules.find(function (b) {
        return b.id === 'yago';
      }) || INITIAL_BARBER_SCHEDULES[1];
      setBarberEditStartTime(yagoSched.startTime);
      setBarberEditEndTime(yagoSched.endTime);
      setBarberEditLunchBreak(yagoSched.lunchBreak);
      setBarberEditActiveDays(yagoSched.activeDays);
      setCurrentView('reception');
      setBarberTab('minha_agenda');
    } else if (roleKey === 'recepcao') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: 'Recepção Valverd\'s',
        email: 'recepcao@valverds.com',
        role: 'recepcao',
        barberKey: 'recepcao',
        title: 'Gerente de Recepção & Caixa',
        avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
      });
      setCurrentView('reception');
      setReceptionTab('agenda');
    } else if (roleKey === 'cliente') {
      setCurrentUser({
        name: clientProfile.name,
        email: clientProfile.email,
        role: 'cliente',
        barberKey: 'cliente',
        title: clientProfile.tier,
        avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
      });
      setCurrentView('client');
      setClientTab('agendamentos');
    }
  };
  var todaysAppointments = useMemo(function () {
    return appointments.filter(function (a) {
      return a.day === 'Seg' && (a.weekId === 0 || a.weekId === undefined);
    });
  }, [appointments]);
  var completedToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'concluido';
  });
  var inChairToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'em_atendimento';
  });
  var waitingToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'aguardando';
  });
  var scheduledToday = todaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'agendado';
  });
  var revenueServices = completedToday.reduce(function (acc, a) {
    return acc + (a.total || a.price);
  }, 0);
  var revenueBalcao = counterSales.reduce(function (acc, s) {
    return acc + s.total;
  }, 0);
  var totalRevenueToday = revenueServices + revenueBalcao;
  var currentBarberName = currentUser.role === 'barbeiro' ? currentUser.name : currentUser.role === 'dono' ? 'Luis Valverde' : '';
  var myAllAppointments = useMemo(function () {
    return appointments.filter(function (a) {
      return a.barber === currentBarberName;
    });
  }, [appointments, currentBarberName]);
  var myWeekAppointments = useMemo(function () {
    return myAllAppointments.filter(function (a) {
      return a.weekId === currentWeek.id || currentWeek.id === 0 && a.weekId === undefined;
    });
  }, [myAllAppointments, currentWeek]);
  var myTodaysAppointments = useMemo(function () {
    return myAllAppointments.filter(function (a) {
      return a.day === 'Seg' && (a.weekId === 0 || a.weekId === undefined);
    });
  }, [myAllAppointments]);
  var myCompletedToday = myTodaysAppointments.filter(function (a) {
    return a.status === 'concluido';
  });
  var myRevenueToday = myCompletedToday.reduce(function (acc, a) {
    return acc + a.price;
  }, 0);
  var myCommissionRate = currentUser.name.includes('Luis') ? 0.60 : 0.50;
  var myCommissionToday = myRevenueToday * myCommissionRate;
  var updateStock = function updateStock(id, delta) {
    setProducts(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (item) {
        return item.id === id ? _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, item), {}, {
          stock: Math.max(0, item.stock + delta)
        }) : item;
      });
    });
  };
  var changeAppointmentStatus = function changeAppointmentStatus(id, newStatus) {
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (a) {
        return a.id === id ? _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, a), {}, {
          status: newStatus
        }) : a;
      });
    });
  };
  var deleteAppointment = function deleteAppointment(id) {
    if (confirm('Deseja realmente cancelar e remover este agendamento?')) {
      setAppointments(function (prev) {
        return prev.filter(function (a) {
          return a.id !== id;
        });
      });
    }
  };
  var openEditAppointment = function openEditAppointment(appt) {
    setEditingAppt(_objectSpread({}, appt));
    setIsEditApptModalOpen(true);
  };
  var handleSaveEditedAppt = function handleSaveEditedAppt(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (a) {
        return a.id === editingAppt.id ? editingAppt : a;
      });
    });
    setIsEditApptModalOpen(false);
  };
  var handleCreateBooking = function handleCreateBooking(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!bookingForm.client || !bookingForm.phone) {
      alert('Por favor preencha nome e WhatsApp.');
      return;
    }
    var srv = services.find(function (s) {
      return s.id === bookingForm.serviceId;
    }) || services[0];
    var brb = barbers.find(function (b) {
      return b.barberKey === bookingForm.barberKey;
    }) || barbers[0];
    var newAppt = {
      id: Date.now(),
      weekId: currentWeek.id,
      day: bookingForm.day,
      date: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? '08/09' : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? '09/09' : '10/09',
      dayNum: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? 8 : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? 9 : 10,
      client: bookingForm.client,
      phone: bookingForm.phone,
      service: srv.name,
      barber: brb.name,
      time: bookingForm.time,
      status: 'agendado',
      price: srv.price,
      items: [{
        name: srv.name,
        price: srv.price
      }],
      total: srv.price,
      paymentMethod: '',
      notes: bookingForm.notes || 'Agendado pelo painel.'
    };
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return [newAppt].concat(_toConsumableArray(prev));
    });
    setIsBookingModalOpen(false);
    setBookingForm({
      client: '',
      phone: '',
      serviceId: 's1',
      barberKey: 'luis',
      day: 'Seg',
      time: '14:00',
      notes: ''
    });
  };
  var openCheckout = function openCheckout(appt) {
    setCheckoutAppt(_objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, appt), {}, {
      items: _toConsumableArray(appt.items)
    }));
    setIsCheckoutModalOpen(true);
  };
  var addItemToCheckout = function addItemToCheckout(product) {
    if (!checkoutAppt || !product) return;
    if (product.price > 0 && product.stock <= 0) {
      alert('Item sem estoque disponível!');
      return;
    }
    updateStock(product.id, -1);
    setCheckoutAppt(function (prev) {
      return _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, prev), {}, {
        items: [].concat(_toConsumableArray(prev.items), [{
          name: product.name,
          price: product.price
        }]),
        total: prev.total + product.price
      });
    });
  };
  var handleConfirmCheckout = function handleConfirmCheckout() {
    if (!checkoutAppt) return;
    setAppointments(function (prev) {
      return prev.map(function (a) {
        return a.id === checkoutAppt.id ? _objectSpread(_objectSpread({}, a), {}, {
          status: 'concluido',
          items: checkoutAppt.items,
          total: checkoutAppt.total,
          paymentMethod: selectedPayment
        }) : a;
      });
    });
    setIsCheckoutModalOpen(false);
    setCheckoutAppt(null);
  };
  var handleQuickSale = function handleQuickSale(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!quickSaleProduct) return;
    var total = quickSaleProduct.price * quickSaleQty;
    updateStock(quickSaleProduct.id, -quickSaleQty);
    setCounterSales(function (prev) {
      return [{
        id: Date.now(),
        item: quickSaleProduct.name,
        category: quickSaleProduct.category,
        qty: quickSaleQty,
        total: total,
        time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', {
          hour: '2-digit',
          minute: '2-digit'
        }),
        paymentMethod: quickSalePayment
      }].concat(_toConsumableArray(prev));
    });
    setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(false);
  };
  var handleAddNewProduct = function handleAddNewProduct(e) {

    setNewProductForm({

      category: 'Bar',
      price: 15,
      stock: 24,
      minStock: 8,
      type: 'Cerveja'
    });
  };

  var toggleBarberDay = function toggleBarberDay(day) {

        return d !== day;
      }));

      setBarberEditActiveDays([].concat(_toConsumableArray(barberEditActiveDays), [day]));
    }
  };

    // =========================================================================
    // COMPONENTE PRINCIPAL
    // =========================================================================
    function ValverdsApp() {
      const [currentView, setCurrentView] = useState('site'); // 'site' | 'reception' | 'client'
      const [activeNav, setActiveNav] = useState('home');

      const [currentUser, setCurrentUser] = useState({
        name: 'Luis Valverde',
        email: 'luis@valverds.com',
        role: 'dono',
        barberKey: 'luis',
        title: 'Barbeiro & Dono',
        avatar: 'luis_valverde.jpg'
      });

      const [receptionTab, setReceptionTab] = useState('agenda');
      const [barberTab, setBarberTab] = useState('minha_agenda');
      const [barberCalendarMode, setBarberCalendarMode] = useState('semanal');
      const [selectedWeekIndex, setSelectedWeekIndex] = useState(1);
      const [selectedWeekDay, setSelectedWeekDay] = useState('Seg');
      const [selectedMonthDay, setSelectedMonthDay] = useState(8);

      const [clientTab, setClientTab] = useState('agendamentos');

      const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState(INITIAL_APPOINTMENTS);
      const [products, setProducts] = useState(INITIAL_PRODUCTS);
      const [services, setServices] = useState(INITIAL_SERVICES);
      const [barbers, setBarbers] = useState(INITIAL_BARBERS);
      const [shopHours, setShopHours] = useState(INITIAL_SHOP_HOURS);
      const [barberSchedules, setBarberSchedules] = useState(INITIAL_BARBER_SCHEDULES);
      const [clientProfile, setClientProfile] = useState(INITIAL_CLIENT_PROFILE);

      const [barberEditStartTime, setBarberEditStartTime] = useState('10:00');
      const [barberEditEndTime, setBarberEditEndTime] = useState('19:00');
      const [barberEditLunchBreak, setBarberEditLunchBreak] = useState('13:00 às 14:00');
      const [barberEditActiveDays, setBarberEditActiveDays] = useState(['Ter', 'Qua', 'Qui', 'Sex', 'Sáb']);
      const [scheduleSavedToast, setScheduleSavedToast] = useState(false);

      const [isBookingModalOpen, setIsBookingModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [isEditApptModalOpen, setIsEditApptModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [editingAppt, setEditingAppt] = useState(null);

      const [isCheckoutModalOpen, setIsCheckoutModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [checkoutAppt, setCheckoutAppt] = useState(null);
      const [selectedPayment, setSelectedPayment] = useState('PIX');
      const [additionalItemId, setAdditionalItemId] = useState('');

      const [isQuickSaleModalOpen, setIsQuickSaleModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [quickSaleProduct, setQuickSaleProduct] = useState(INITIAL_PRODUCTS[0]);
      const [quickSaleQty, setQuickSaleQty] = useState(1);
      const [quickSalePayment, setQuickSalePayment] = useState('PIX');

      const [isNewProductModalOpen, setIsNewProductModalOpen] = useState(false);
      const [newProductForm, setNewProductForm] = useState({ name: '', category: 'Bar', price: 15, stock: 24, minStock: 8, type: 'Cerveja' });

      const [bookingForm, setBookingForm] = useState({
        client: '',
        phone: '',
        serviceId: 's1',
        barberKey: 'luis',
        day: 'Seg',
        time: '14:00',
        notes: ''
      });

      const [counterSales, setCounterSales] = useState([
        { id: 901, item: 'Pomada Modeladora Matte Valverd\'s (150g)', category: 'Produtos', qty: 1, total: 45, time: '10:15', paymentMethod: 'PIX' },
        { id: 902, item: 'Cerveja IPA Artesanal Valverd\'s (500ml)', category: 'Bar', qty: 2, total: 40, time: '11:20', paymentMethod: 'CARTÃO' }
      ]);

      const [currentSlide, setCurrentSlide] = useState(0);
      const currentWeek = WEEKS_DATA[selectedWeekIndex] || WEEKS_DATA[1];
      const nextSlide = () => setCurrentSlide((prev) => (prev + 1) % GALLERY_SLIDES.length);
      const prevSlide = () => setCurrentSlide((prev) => (prev - 1 + GALLERY_SLIDES.length) % GALLERY_SLIDES.length);

      const scrollToSection = (id) => {
        setActiveNav(id);
        if (currentView !== 'site') setCurrentView('site');
        const element = document.getElementById(id);
        if (element) element.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
      };

      const switchRole = (roleKey) => {
        if (roleKey === 'dono') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: 'Luis Valverde',
            email: 'luis@valverds.com',
            role: 'dono',
            barberKey: 'luis',
            title: 'Barbeiro & Dono',
            avatar: 'luis_valverde.jpg'
          });
          setCurrentView('reception');
          setReceptionTab('agenda');
        } else if (roleKey === 'barbeiro') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: 'Yago Barber',
            email: 'yago@valverds.com',
            role: 'barbeiro',
            barberKey: 'yago',
            title: 'Barbeiro',
            avatar: 'yago_barber.png'
          });
          const yagoSched = barberSchedules.find(b => b.id === 'yago') || INITIAL_BARBER_SCHEDULES[1];
          setBarberEditStartTime(yagoSched.startTime);
          setBarberEditEndTime(yagoSched.endTime);
          setBarberEditLunchBreak(yagoSched.lunchBreak);
          setBarberEditActiveDays(yagoSched.activeDays);
          setCurrentView('reception');
          setBarberTab('minha_agenda');
        } else if (roleKey === 'recepcao') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: 'Recepção Valverd\'s',
            email: 'recepcao@valverds.com',
            role: 'recepcao',
            barberKey: 'recepcao',
            title: 'Gerente de Recepção & Caixa',
            avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
          });
          setCurrentView('reception');
          setReceptionTab('agenda');
        } else if (roleKey === 'cliente') {
          setCurrentUser({
            name: clientProfile.name,
            email: clientProfile.email,
            role: 'cliente',
            barberKey: 'cliente',
            title: clientProfile.tier,
            avatar: 'logo_valverds_clean.png'
          });
          setCurrentView('client');
          setClientTab('agendamentos');
        }
      };

      const todaysAppointments = useMemo(() => {
        return appointments.filter(a => a.day === 'Seg' && (a.weekId === 0 || a.weekId === undefined));
      }, [appointments]);

      const completedToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'concluido');
      const inChairToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'em_atendimento');
      const waitingToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'aguardando');
      const scheduledToday = todaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'agendado');

      const revenueServices = completedToday.reduce((acc, a) => acc + (a.total || a.price), 0);
      const revenueBalcao = counterSales.reduce((acc, s) => acc + s.total, 0);
      const totalRevenueToday = revenueServices + revenueBalcao;

      const currentBarberName = currentUser.role === 'barbeiro' ? currentUser.name : (currentUser.role === 'dono' ? 'Luis Valverde' : '');
      const myAllAppointments = useMemo(() => appointments.filter(a => a.barber === currentBarberName), [appointments, currentBarberName]);
      const myWeekAppointments = useMemo(() => myAllAppointments.filter(a => a.weekId === currentWeek.id || (currentWeek.id === 0 && a.weekId === undefined)), [myAllAppointments, currentWeek]);
      const myTodaysAppointments = useMemo(() => myAllAppointments.filter(a => a.day === 'Seg' && (a.weekId === 0 || a.weekId === undefined)), [myAllAppointments]);
      const myCompletedToday = myTodaysAppointments.filter(a => a.status === 'concluido');
      const myRevenueToday = myCompletedToday.reduce((acc, a) => acc + a.price, 0);
      const myCommissionRate = currentUser.name.includes('Luis') ? 0.60 : 0.50;
      const myCommissionToday = myRevenueToday * myCommissionRate;

      const updateStock = (id, delta) => {
        setProducts(prev => prev.map(item => item.id === id ? { ...item, stock: Math.max(0, item.stock + delta) } : item));
      };

      const changeAppointmentStatus = (id, newStatus) => {
        setAppointments(prev => prev.map(a => a.id === id ? { ...a, status: newStatus } : a));
      };

      const deleteAppointment = (id) => {
        if (confirm('Deseja realmente cancelar e remover este agendamento?')) {
          setAppointments(prev => prev.filter(a => a.id !== id));
        }
      };

      const openEditAppointment = (appt) => {
        setEditingAppt({ ...appt });
        setIsEditApptModalOpen(true);
      };

      const handleSaveEditedAppt = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        setAppointments(prev => prev.map(a => a.id === editingAppt.id ? editingAppt : a));
        setIsEditApptModalOpen(false);
      };

      const handleCreateBooking = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!bookingForm.client || !bookingForm.phone) {
          alert('Por favor preencha nome e WhatsApp.');
          return;
        }

        const srv = services.find(s => s.id === bookingForm.serviceId) || services[0];
        const brb = barbers.find(b => b.barberKey === bookingForm.barberKey) || barbers[0];

        const newAppt = {
          id: Date.now(),
          weekId: currentWeek.id,
          day: bookingForm.day,
          date: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? '08/09' : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? '09/09' : '10/09',
          dayNum: bookingForm.day === 'Seg' ? 8 : bookingForm.day === 'Ter' ? 9 : 10,
          client: bookingForm.client,
          phone: bookingForm.phone,
          service: srv.name,
          barber: brb.name,
          time: bookingForm.time,
          status: 'agendado',
          price: srv.price,
          items: [{ name: srv.name, price: srv.price }],
          total: srv.price,
          paymentMethod: '',
          notes: bookingForm.notes || 'Agendado pelo painel.'
        };

        setAppointments(prev => [newAppt, ...prev]);
        setIsBookingModalOpen(false);
        setBookingForm({ client: '', phone: '', serviceId: 's1', barberKey: 'luis', day: 'Seg', time: '14:00', notes: '' });
      };

      const openCheckout = (appt) => {
        setCheckoutAppt({ ...appt, items: [...appt.items] });
        setIsCheckoutModalOpen(true);
      };

      const addItemToCheckout = (product) => {
        if (!checkoutAppt || !product) return;
        if (product.price > 0 && product.stock <= 0) {
          alert('Item sem estoque disponível!');
          return;
        }
        updateStock(product.id, -1);
        setCheckoutAppt(prev => ({
          ...prev,
          items: [...prev.items, { name: product.name, price: product.price }],
          total: prev.total + product.price
        }));
      };

      const handleConfirmCheckout = () => {
        if (!checkoutAppt) return;
        setAppointments(prev => prev.map(a => a.id === checkoutAppt.id ? {
          ...a,
          status: 'concluido',
          items: checkoutAppt.items,
          total: checkoutAppt.total,
          paymentMethod: selectedPayment
        } : a));
        setIsCheckoutModalOpen(false);
        setCheckoutAppt(null);
      };

      const handleQuickSale = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (!quickSaleProduct) return;
        const total = quickSaleProduct.price * quickSaleQty;
        updateStock(quickSaleProduct.id, -quickSaleQty);
        setCounterSales(prev => [
          {
            id: Date.now(),
            item: quickSaleProduct.name,
            category: quickSaleProduct.category,
            qty: quickSaleQty,
            total,
            time: new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
            paymentMethod: quickSalePayment
          },
          ...prev
        ]);
        setIsQuickSaleModalOpen(false);
      };

      const handleAddNewProduct = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const newP = {
          id: 'p' + Date.now(),
          name: newProductForm.name,
          category: newProductForm.category,
          price: parseFloat(newProductForm.price),
          stock: parseInt(newProductForm.stock),
          minStock: parseInt(newProductForm.minStock),
          type: newProductForm.type
        };
        setProducts(prev => [...prev, newP]);
        setIsNewProductModalOpen(false);
        setNewProductForm({ name: '', category: 'Bar', price: 15, stock: 24, minStock: 8, type: 'Cerveja' });
      };

      const handleSaveBarberSchedule = (e) => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const currentBarberId = currentUser.barberKey || 'yago';
        setBarberSchedules(prev => prev.map(b => b.id === currentBarberId ? {
          ...b,
          startTime: barberEditStartTime,
          endTime: barberEditEndTime,
          lunchBreak: barberEditLunchBreak,
          activeDays: barberEditActiveDays,
          workDaysDesc: `${barberEditActiveDays.join(', ')} (${barberEditStartTime} às ${barberEditEndTime})`
        } : b));
        setScheduleSavedToast(true);
        setTimeout(() => setScheduleSavedToast(false), 4000);
      };

      const toggleBarberDay = (day) => {
        if (barberEditActiveDays.includes(day)) {
          setBarberEditActiveDays(barberEditActiveDays.filter(d => d !== day));
        } else {
          setBarberEditActiveDays([...barberEditActiveDays, day]);
        }
      };

      const StatusBadge = ({ status }) => {
        const styles = {
          concluido: 'bg-emerald-950/60 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-700/50',
          em_atendimento: 'bg-blue-950/60 text-blue-300 border-blue-700/50',
          aguardando: 'bg-amber-950/60 text-amber-300 border-amber-700/50',
          agendado: 'bg-[#1E1D19] text-[#A8A397] border-[#2A261F]'
        };
        const labels = {
          concluido: 'Concluído',
          em_atendimento: 'Na Cadeira',
          aguardando: 'No Lounge',
          agendado: 'Agendado'
        };
        return (
          <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-serif font-bold uppercase tracking-wider border ${styles[status] || styles.agendado}`}>
            <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full mr-1.5 ${status === 'concluido' ? 'bg-emerald-400' : status === 'em_atendimento' ? 'bg-blue-400' : status === 'aguardando' ? 'bg-amber-400' : 'bg-[#C9A356]'}`}></span>
            {labels[status] || status}
          </span>
        );
      };

      const DemoRoleSwitcher = () => (
        <div className="bg-[#191713] border border-[#3A3326] rounded-full p-1 flex items-center gap-1 shadow-2xl">
          <span className="text-[10px] font-serif uppercase tracking-widest text-[#C9A356] font-bold px-2.5 hidden sm:inline">
            PERFIL DEMO:
          </span>
          <button
            onClick={() => switchRole('dono')}
            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${currentUser.role === 'dono' && currentView === 'reception' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-[#25221B]'}`}
            title="Dono: Acesso irrestrito a DRE, todas as cadeiras e equipe"
          >
            <Icons.Crown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Dono</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => switchRole('barbeiro')}
            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${currentUser.role === 'barbeiro' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-[#25221B]'}`}
            title="Barbeiro (Yago): Minha Agenda, Meus Ganhos, Minha Escala"
          >
            <Icons.Scissors className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Barbeiro</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => switchRole('recepcao')}
            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${currentUser.role === 'recepcao' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-[#25221B]'}`}
            title="Recepção: Fila do Sofá e Encaixes"
          >
            <Icons.Clipboard className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Recepção</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => switchRole('cliente')}
            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-[11px] font-bold transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 ${currentView === 'client' ? 'bg-[#C9A356] text-black shadow' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-[#25221B]'}`}
            title="Cliente VIP: Cartão Fidelidade e Agendamento"
          >
            <Icons.User className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Cliente</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      );

      return (
        <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#0B0B0B] text-[#E0DDD5] font-sans antialiased flex flex-col selection:bg-[#C9A356] selection:text-black">
          
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {/* MODO 1: SITE PÚBLICO                                                      */}
          {/* ========================================================================= */}
          {currentView === 'site' && (
            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row min-h-screen">
              
              {/* SIDEBAR VERTICAL ESQUERDA REFINADA */}
              <aside className="w-full md:w-[140px] md:fixed md:inset-y-0 md:left-0 bg-[#0B0B0B] border-r border-[#211F1C] z-40 flex md:flex-col items-center justify-between p-3 md:py-6 text-white shadow-2xl">
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center cursor-pointer mb-3" onClick={() => scrollToSection('home')}>
                  <OfficialLogo className="w-16 h-16" />
                  <span className="text-[8px] font-serif tracking-[0.25em] text-[#C9A356] mt-1 font-bold">EST. 2022</span>
                </div>

                <nav className="w-full flex md:flex-col items-center justify-around md:justify-center gap-1 md:gap-2 py-1">
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('home')} className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-2 rounded-xl w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'home' ? 'text-[#C9A356] bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A2419]' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#161410]/50'}`}>
                    <Icons.Home className="w-4 h-4 mb-1" />
                    <span className="text-[8px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-bold">HOME</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('sobre-nos')} className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-2 rounded-xl w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'sobre-nos' ? 'text-[#C9A356] bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A2419]' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#161410]/50'}`}>
                    <Icons.Info className="w-4 h-4 mb-1" />
                    <span className="text-[8px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-bold">SOBRE</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('espaco')} className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-2 rounded-xl w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'espaco' ? 'text-[#C9A356] bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A2419]' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#161410]/50'}`}>
                    <Icons.Camera className="w-4 h-4 mb-1" />
                    <span className="text-[8px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-bold">ESPAÇO</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('servicos')} className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-2 rounded-xl w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'servicos' ? 'text-[#C9A356] bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A2419]' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#161410]/50'}`}>
                    <Icons.Scissors className="w-4 h-4 mb-1" />
                    <span className="text-[8px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-bold">SERVIÇOS</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('bebidas')} className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-2 rounded-xl w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'bebidas' ? 'text-[#C9A356] bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A2419]' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#161410]/50'}`}>
                    <Icons.Wine className="w-4 h-4 mb-1" />
                    <span className="text-[8px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-bold">BAR</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('barbeiros')} className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-2 rounded-xl w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'barbeiros' ? 'text-[#C9A356] bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A2419]' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#161410]/50'}`}>
                    <Icons.Users className="w-4 h-4 mb-1" />
                    <span className="text-[8px] font-serif tracking-widest uppercase font-bold">EQUIPE</span>
                  </button>
                  <button onClick={() => scrollToSection('localizacao')} className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-2 rounded-xl w-full transition-all ${activeNav === 'localizacao' ? 'text-[#C9A356] bg-[#161410] border border-[#2A2419]' : 'text-gray-400 hover:text-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#161410]/50'}`}>
                    <Icons.MapPin className="w-4 h-4 mb-1 text-[#C9A356]" />
                    <span className="text-[8px] font-serif tracking-wider uppercase font-bold">RESENDE</span>
                  </button>

                                <div className="w-full pt-3 border-t border-[#211F1C] flex flex-col gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={handleInstallApp}
                    className="w-full py-2 px-1 rounded-xl bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-black font-bold text-[8px] tracking-wider uppercase transition-all flex flex-col items-center text-center shadow"
                    title="Instalar App na tela do celular"
                  >
                    <Icons.Package className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mb-0.5" />
                    <span>INSTALAR APP</span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => switchRole('cliente')}
                    className="w-full py-2 px-1 rounded-xl bg-[#C9A356]/15 hover:bg-[#C9A356] border border-[#C9A356]/40 text-[#C9A356] hover:text-black transition-all flex flex-col items-center text-center shadow"
                  >
                    <Icons.User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mb-0.5" />
                    <span className="text-[8px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">ÁREA CLIENTE</span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    onClick={() => switchRole('dono')}
                    className="w-full py-2 px-1 rounded-xl bg-[#161512] hover:bg-[#201E19] border border-[#2A261F] text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition-all flex flex-col items-center text-center"
                  >
                    <Icons.Lock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 mb-0.5" />

                  </button>

                  <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto flex flex-col items-center z-10 text-white">
                    <div className="w-44 h-44 sm:w-52 sm:h-52 rounded-full p-2 bg-[#0B0B0B]/90 border-2 border-[#C9A356]/60 shadow-2xl flex items-center justify-center backdrop-blur-md mb-5 hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                      <OfficialLogo className="w-full h-full" />
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 border border-[#C9A356]/50 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full mb-3 bg-[#0B0B0B]/80">
                      <Icons.MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#C9A356]" />
                      <span className="text-[11px] font-serif uppercase tracking-[0.25em] text-[#E0DDD5]">R. ANTÔNIO VELOSO JÚNIOR, 77 • CAMPOS ELÍSEOS • RESENDE / RJ</span>
                    </div>

                      VALVERD'S
                    </h1>
                    <div className="text-base sm:text-xl font-serif italic text-[#C9A356] tracking-widest mb-3">
                      BarberShop — Desde 2022
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-sm sm:text-base md:text-lg font-serif text-[#D6D2CA] tracking-widest uppercase max-w-xl mb-2">
                      "Beard, hair and mustache"
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-[#C9A356] font-serif font-bold tracking-wider uppercase mb-8">
                      "Dê um tapa no seu estilo!"
                    </p>

                    <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-4 items-center">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => { setBookingForm(prev => ({ ...prev, serviceId: 's1', barberKey: 'luis' })); setIsBookingModalOpen(true); }}
                        className="px-8 py-3.5 bg-[#C9A356] hover:bg-[#B59044] text-[#0B0B0B] font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest rounded-xl shadow-xl transition-all hover:scale-105"
                      >

                      <button
                        onClick={() => switchRole('cliente')}
                        className="px-7 py-3.5 bg-[#161512] hover:bg-[#201E19] text-[#C9A356] border border-[#C9A356]/50 font-serif text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest rounded-xl shadow-xl transition-all flex items-center gap-2"
                      >
                        <Icons.User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#C9A356]" />
                        <span>Meu Espaço VIP / Fidelidade</span>

                {/* PILARES */}
                <section className="bg-[#12110E] text-white py-14 px-6 border-y border-[#211F1C]">
                  <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
                    {BRAND_PILLARS.map((p, idx) => {
                      const IconComp = Icons[p.icon] || Icons.Scissors;
                      return (

                          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-[#1C1A14] border border-[#C9A356]/30 flex items-center justify-center mb-3 text-[#C9A356]">
                            <IconComp className="w-5 h-5" />
                          </div>
                          <h3 className="font-serif font-bold text-sm uppercase text-[#C9A356] mb-2">{p.title}</h3>
                          <p className="text-xs text-[#9E9B93] leading-relaxed">{p.desc}</p>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                </section>

                {/* SOBRE NÓS */}
                <section id="sobre-nos" className="py-24 px-6 md:px-16 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-12 items-center">
                    <div className="relative aspect-[4/5] rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl border border-[#282319]">
                      <img src="luis_valverde.jpg" alt="Luis Valverde na Valverd's BarberShop" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                      <div className="absolute bottom-0 inset-x-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/90 via-black/40 to-transparent p-6 text-white">
                        <div className="text-[#C9A356] font-serif text-xs uppercase font-bold tracking-wider">Barbearia Valverd's</div>
                        <div className="font-serif text-xl font-bold">Luis Valverde (@luisvalverdelv)</div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <div className="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-[#161512] border border-[#C9A356]/30 inline-block mb-4 text-[#C9A356]">
                        <Icons.Scissors className="w-8 h-8" />
                      </div>
                      <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl sm:text-4xl font-bold tracking-[0.2em] uppercase text-white mb-6">S O B R E - N Ó S</h2>
                      <p className="text-[#B5B0A4] leading-relaxed text-sm sm:text-base mb-4">
